===== additional.py SHA256 8c5e8a37099be6ba7852cc245ab1319f6473b788bfd6a80ba2b1b040d811a107 =====
from pathlib import Path
import os,subprocess,json,shlex
root=Path.cwd();w=root/".deps/p2a-astra-final"
e=os.environ.copy();e.update(CARGO_HOME=str(w/"probe-home"),CARGO_TARGET_DIR=str(w/"additional-target"),CARGO_CACHE_RUSTC_INFO="0")
a=["--offline","--locked","--manifest-path","trusted-client/Cargo.toml"]
results=[]
def run(name,c,success,env=e):
 p=subprocess.run(c,env=env,cwd=root,capture_output=True,text=True)
 (w/(name+".log")).write_text("ENV "+repr({k:env[k] for k in ("RUSTFLAGS","CARGO_CACHE_RUSTC_INFO") if k in env})+"\n$ "+shlex.join(map(str,c))+"\n"+p.stdout+p.stderr+"\nexit="+str(p.returncode)+"\n")
 results.append(dict(name=name,command=list(map(str,c)),exit=p.returncode));(w/"additional-results.json").write_text(json.dumps(results,indent=2))
 assert (p.returncode==0)==success,(name,p.returncode,p.stderr)
 print(name,p.returncode,flush=True)
 return p
for b in ("linux_getrandom","custom","linux_raw","rdrand","rndr","efi_rng","windows_legacy","unsupported","extern_impl"):
 p=run("uncached-clippy-"+b,["cargo","clippy",*a,"--","--cfg",f'getrandom_backend="{b}"'],False)
 assert "Phase 2A rustc policy rejected" in p.stderr
p=run("uncached-clippy-response",["cargo","clippy",*a,"--","@"+str(w/"independent-duplicate.rsp")],False)
assert "Phase 2A rustc policy rejected" in p.stderr
run("uncached-clippy-warning-positive",["cargo","clippy",*a,"--all-targets","--","-D","warnings","-W","clippy::all"],True)
run("independent-explicit-target",["cargo","check",*a,"--target","x86_64-unknown-linux-gnu"],True)
p=run("independent-rustdoc-response",["cargo","rustdoc",*a,"--","@"+str(w/"independent-duplicate.rsp")],False)
assert "Phase 2A rustdoc policy rejected" in p.stderr
for name,extra in [("duplicate",['--cfg=getrandom_backend="linux_getrandom"']),("custom",['--cfg=getrandom_backend="custom"']),("response",['@'+str(w/"independent-duplicate.rsp")])]:
 p=run("cargo-query-"+name,["cargo","rustc",*a,"--","--print=link-args",*extra],False)
 assert "Phase 2A rustc policy rejected" in p.stderr
p=run("cargo-query-missing",["cargo","rustc",*a,"--","--print=link-args"],False,dict(e,RUSTFLAGS="",CARGO_TARGET_DIR=str(w/"missing-target")))
assert "Phase 2A rustc policy rejected" in p.stderr
assert not list((w/"missing-target").rglob("libcadp_pqc*.rlib"))

===== cache_repro.py SHA256 93adb38d0879d318c35b6c22e0af2cd73882d35674ead937c3ef18417a63a79a =====
from pathlib import Path
import os,subprocess,json,shlex,hashlib
root=Path.cwd();w=root/".deps/p2a-astra-final";target=w/"clippy-existing-cache-target"
assert not target.exists()
target.mkdir()
e=os.environ.copy();e.update(CARGO_HOME=str(w/"probe-home"),CARGO_TARGET_DIR=str(target))
for k in ("CLIPPY_ARGS","CARGO_CACHE_RUSTC_INFO","RUSTFLAGS","CARGO_ENCODED_RUSTFLAGS","RUSTC_WRAPPER","RUSTC_WORKSPACE_WRAPPER"):e.pop(k,None)
base=["cargo","clippy","--offline","--locked","--manifest-path","trusted-client/Cargo.toml"]
bad=[*base,"--","@"+str(w/"independent-duplicate.rsp")]
good=[*base,"--all-targets","--","-D","warnings"]
results=[]
def run(name,c):
 p=subprocess.run(c,env=e,capture_output=True,text=True)
 (w/(name+".log")).write_text("$ "+shlex.join(c)+"\n"+p.stdout+p.stderr+"\nexit="+str(p.returncode)+"\n")
 cache=target/".rustc_info.json"
 cache_contents=cache.read_text() if cache.exists() else None
 results.append(dict(name=name,command=c,exit=p.returncode,cache=cache_contents))
 (w/"cache-repro-results.json").write_text(json.dumps(results,indent=2));print(name,p.returncode,flush=True)
 return p
assert run("cache-01-bad",bad).returncode!=0
assert run("cache-02-normal",good).returncode!=0
(target/".rustc_info.json").rename(w/"failed-rustc-info.json")
assert run("cache-03-normal-after-reset",good).returncode==0
p=run("cache-04-bad-after-good",bad)
print("BAD_AFTER_GOOD_ACCEPTED",p.returncode==0,flush=True)
run("cache-05-normal-after-warm-bad",good)

===== finish_core.py SHA256 12c7b89e5232fc025b2582a0c1b1d575729bc4e09314558545e78de595f01437 =====
from pathlib import Path
import os,subprocess,json,time,shlex,concurrent.futures
w=Path(__file__).resolve().parent;root=w.parent.parent
a=['--offline','--locked','--manifest-path','trusted-client/Cargo.toml']
cmds={4:['python3','scripts/check_rng_policy.py'],5:['python3','scripts/validate_phase2a.py'],6:['python3','scripts/validate_phase1.py'],7:['make','build'],8:['make','check'],9:['git','diff','--check']}
prior=json.loads((w/'core-results.json').read_text());assert len(prior)==4
results={i:r for i,r in enumerate(prior)}
def run(i):
 e=os.environ.copy();e.update(CARGO_HOME=str(w/'cargo-home'),CARGO_TARGET_DIR=str(w/(f'finish-target-{i}' if i in (4,5,8) else 'target')))
 t=time.monotonic();c=cmds[i]
 with (w/f'core-{i:02}.log').open('w') as f:
  f.write('ENV '+json.dumps({k:e[k] for k in ('CARGO_HOME','CARGO_TARGET_DIR')})+'\n$ '+shlex.join(c)+'\n');f.flush()
  p=subprocess.run(c,cwd=root,env=e,stdout=f,stderr=subprocess.STDOUT)
  f.write('\nexit='+str(p.returncode)+'\n')
 return i,dict(command=c,exit=p.returncode,seconds=round(time.monotonic()-t,2),environment={k:e[k] for k in ('CARGO_HOME','CARGO_TARGET_DIR')})
with concurrent.futures.ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=4) as pool:
 for future in concurrent.futures.as_completed([pool.submit(run,i) for i in cmds]):
  i,r=future.result();results[i]=r
  (w/'finish-results.json').write_text(json.dumps(results,indent=2));print(i,r,flush=True)
assert len(results)==10
(w/'core-results.json').write_text(json.dumps([results[i] for i in range(10)],indent=2))

===== independent.py SHA256 61a01186295ad51e29928ac73e0f2b7cc1d5452a726f36c307429ecaeedb06f0 =====
from pathlib import Path
import os,subprocess,json,hashlib,shlex
root=Path.cwd();w=root/".deps/p2a-astra-final";e=os.environ.copy();e.update(CARGO_HOME=str(w/"probe-home"),CARGO_TARGET_DIR=str(w/"probe-target"))
for k in ("RUSTFLAGS","CARGO_ENCODED_RUSTFLAGS","RUSTDOCFLAGS","CARGO_ENCODED_RUSTDOCFLAGS","RUSTC_WRAPPER","RUSTC_WORKSPACE_WRAPPER"):e.pop(k,None)
a=["--offline","--locked","--manifest-path","trusted-client/Cargo.toml"]
report=[]
def run(label,c,expect,env=e):
 p=subprocess.run(c,cwd=root,env=env,capture_output=True,text=True)
 (w/(label+".log")).write_text("$ "+shlex.join(map(str,c))+"\n"+p.stdout+p.stderr+"\nexit="+str(p.returncode)+"\n")
 assert (p.returncode==0)==expect,(label,p.returncode,p.stderr)
 report.append(dict(label=label,command=list(map(str,c)),exit=p.returncode))
 (w/"independent-results.json").write_text(json.dumps(report,indent=2))
 print(label,p.returncode,flush=True)
 return p
rsp=w/"independent-duplicate.rsp";rsp.write_text('--cfg=getrandom_backend="linux_getrandom"\n')
for stage in ("fresh","reused"):
 before={str(p):hashlib.sha256(p.read_bytes()).hexdigest() for p in (w/"probe-target").rglob("libcadp_pqc*.rlib")}
 p=run("independent-duplicate-"+stage,["cargo","rustc",*a,"--","@"+str(rsp)],False)
 assert "Phase 2A rustc policy rejected" in p.stderr and "--crate-name cadp_pqc" in p.stderr
 after={str(p):hashlib.sha256(p.read_bytes()).hexdigest() for p in (w/"probe-target").rglob("libcadp_pqc*.rlib")}
 assert before==after
 if stage=="fresh":
  assert not before and not list((w/"probe-target").rglob("libcadp_pqc*.rmeta"))
  run("independent-approved-build",["cargo","build",*a],True)
  assert list((w/"probe-target").rglob("libcadp_pqc*.rlib"))
for backend in ("linux_getrandom","custom","linux_raw","rdrand","rndr","efi_rng","windows_legacy","unsupported","extern_impl"):
 p=run("clippy-"+backend,["cargo","clippy",*a,"--","--cfg",f'getrandom_backend="{backend}"'],False)
 assert "Phase 2A rustc policy rejected" in p.stderr
p=run("clippy-response",["cargo","clippy",*a,"--","@"+str(rsp)],False)
assert "Phase 2A rustc policy rejected" in p.stderr
run("clippy-warning-positive",["cargo","clippy",*a,"--all-targets","--","-D","warnings","-W","clippy::all"],True)
run("independent-explicit-target",["cargo","check",*a,"--target","x86_64-unknown-linux-gnu"],True)
p=run("independent-rustdoc-response",["cargo","rustdoc",*a,"--","@"+str(rsp)],False)
assert "Phase 2A rustdoc policy rejected" in p.stderr

===== resume_core.py SHA256 f2ed8c58f40401036fe534bb0aa64dc31929a0e30a0e707cf82f49538babbb9e =====
from pathlib import Path
import os,subprocess,json,time,shlex
w=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
root=w.parent.parent
env=os.environ.copy()
env.update(CARGO_HOME=str(w/"cargo-home"),CARGO_TARGET_DIR=str(w/"target"))
assert Path(env["CARGO_HOME"]).exists() and Path(env["CARGO_TARGET_DIR"]).exists()
(w/"env.json").write_text(json.dumps({k:env[k] for k in ("CARGO_HOME","CARGO_TARGET_DIR")},indent=2))
a=["--offline","--locked","--manifest-path","trusted-client/Cargo.toml"]
commands=[["cargo","fmt","--manifest-path","trusted-client/Cargo.toml","--check"],["cargo","clippy",*a,"--all-targets","--","-D","warnings"],["cargo","test",*a],["cargo","test",*a,"--release"],["python3","scripts/check_rng_policy.py"],["python3","scripts/validate_phase2a.py"],["python3","scripts/validate_phase1.py"],["make","build"],["make","check"],["git","diff","--check"]]
results=json.loads((w/"core-results.json").read_text())
assert len(results)==4 and all(x["exit"]==0 for x in results)
for i,c in list(enumerate(commands))[4:]:
 t=time.monotonic()
 with (w/f"core-{i:02}.log").open("w") as f:
  f.write("$ "+shlex.join(c)+"\n");f.flush()
  p=subprocess.run(c,cwd=root,env=env,stdout=f,stderr=subprocess.STDOUT)
  f.write("\nexit="+str(p.returncode)+"\n")
 item=dict(command=c,exit=p.returncode,seconds=round(time.monotonic()-t,2))

===== run_core.py SHA256 49bb720470a95693bbb987db30b7829304c33fb1e2cdb0554d167a82528d6a3d =====
from pathlib import Path
import os,subprocess,json,time,shlex
w=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
root=w.parent.parent
env=os.environ.copy()
env.update(CARGO_HOME=str(w/"cargo-home"),CARGO_TARGET_DIR=str(w/"target"))
assert not Path(env["CARGO_HOME"]).exists() and not Path(env["CARGO_TARGET_DIR"]).exists()
(w/"env.json").write_text(json.dumps({k:env[k] for k in ("CARGO_HOME","CARGO_TARGET_DIR")},indent=2))
a=["--offline","--locked","--manifest-path","trusted-client/Cargo.toml"]
commands=[["cargo","fmt","--manifest-path","trusted-client/Cargo.toml","--check"],["cargo","clippy",*a,"--all-targets","--","-D","warnings"],["cargo","test",*a],["cargo","test",*a,"--release"],["python3","scripts/check_rng_policy.py"],["python3","scripts/validate_phase2a.py"],["python3","scripts/validate_phase1.py"],["make","build"],["make","check"],["git","diff","--check"]]
results=[]
for i,c in enumerate(commands):
 t=time.monotonic()
 with (w/f"core-{i:02}.log").open("w") as f:
  f.write("$ "+shlex.join(c)+"\n");f.flush()
  p=subprocess.run(c,cwd=root,env=env,stdout=f,stderr=subprocess.STDOUT)
  f.write("\nexit="+str(p.returncode)+"\n")
 item=dict(command=c,exit=p.returncode,seconds=round(time.monotonic()-t,2))
 results.append(item);(w/"core-results.json").write_text(json.dumps(results,indent=2))
 print(item,flush=True)
